# Sound cue sheet

Generated by `bun scripts/sound-cue-sheet.ts` from the cue tables in this folder, `src/lib/fx/manifest.ts` and the
puzzle registry; `sound-manifest-check` proves it matches the manifest. The same rows are in
[cue-sheet.csv](cue-sheet.csv) with an empty **new file** column to fill in. How to add a file: [README.md](README.md).

One row per cue. **Status** is what plays today: *recorded sound* (a file plays; *(shared)* means another
game's or the app's file stands in until the cue's own slot gets one), *synthesised placeholder* (a
generated tone) or *silent*. **How often** is an estimate for a typical round. **Suggested length** is the
moment's on-screen length, capped at 6 s for one-shots. **Manifest slot** is the entry in
`src/lib/fx/manifest.ts` whose `url` a new file goes on.

Recorded files are hosted by Lovable (`/__l5e/assets-v1/<id>/<name>.mp3`) or, when they came in through git,
kept in `public/sounds/` (`/sounds/<recording>.mp3`), one file per recording: several slots may name the same
file. Every file is listed under [Recorded files](#recorded-files). **What it looks like** describes the arcade
effects ([arcade-effects.md](arcade-effects.md)) so a sound can be written for each.

| Category | Silent | Placeholder | Recorded | Total |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| game event | 35 | 0 | 474 | 509 |
| room moment | 1 | 0 | 41 | 42 |
| music | 10 | 0 | 105 | 115 |
| UI tap | 10 | 0 | 30 | 40 |
| fail sting | 4 | 0 | 37 | 41 |
| success sting | 3 | 0 | 73 | 76 |
| ambience | 0 | 1 | 17 | 18 |
| replay | 0 | 0 | 186 | 186 |
| **all** | 63 | 1 | 963 | 1027 |

### Music: screens

| cue id | where and when it fires | what it looks like | how often in a typical round | status | file it uses today | suggested length (ms) | category | manifest slot |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `music.home` | Home / join (/), and since 1 Nov 2026 the app-wide home bed: Play, Practice's list, Tournaments, Profile and the settings sheet: music_home (music_menu.mp3) |  | loop | recorded sound | `music_home` | 60000 | music | `music_home` |
| `music.practice` | Practice game list (/practice): music_home (the home bed carries on; the slot is not started since 1 Nov 2026) |  | loop | recorded sound | `music_practice` | 60000 | music | `music_practice` |
| `music.lobby` | Room lobby: lobby_loop (crossfades the home bed out over 400 ms; leaving the lobby for a tab page crossfades it out and the home bed resumes where it was) |  | loop | recorded sound | `lobby_loop` | loop | music | `lobby_loop` |
| `music.intro` | Round intro (rooms and practice): intro_loop |  | loop | recorded sound | `intro_loop` | loop | music | `intro_loop` |
| `music.standings` | Round result and standings: standings_loop |  | loop | recorded sound | `standings_loop` | loop | music | `standings_loop` |
| `music.podium` | Podium / final result: podium_loop |  | loop | recorded sound | `podium_loop` | loop | music | `podium_loop` |
